We thank Andrei Barvinsky for suggesting this constructive proof.

We begin with the defining relation

\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}={\hskip 1.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral{I}\hskip 1.0pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt}
as an equation on
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}
.
Using (
163
),

\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}+|1\rangle\tfrac{1}{{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}V}}{\langle 1|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}}\;-\;|W^{-1}\rangle\tfrac{1}{{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}V}}{\langle W|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}}\;=\;{\hskip 1.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral{I}\hskip 1.0pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt}\,.

(171)

Let
{\langle X|}\defeq\langle 1|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}
and
{\langle Y|}\defeq\langle W|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}
. To determine an explicit form of
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}
, we solve for
\langle X|
and
\langle Y|
.
Contracting (
171
) with
\langle W|
gives

{\langle Y|}+\langle W|1\rangle\tfrac{1}{{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}V}}\langle X|-{{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}V}\tfrac{1}{{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}V}}\langle Y|}\;=\;\langle W|,
from which we obtain

\langle X|\;=\;{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}V}\tfrac{1}{\langle W|1\rangle}\langle W|\,.

(172)

Contracting (
171
) with
\langle 1|
yields

\langle X|+{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}V}\tfrac{1}{{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}V}}\langle X|-\langle 1|W^{-1}\rangle\tfrac{1}{{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}V}}\langle Y|\;=\;\langle 1|,
leading to

\langle Y|\;=\;-{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}V}\tfrac{1}{\langle 1|W^{-1}\rangle}\big(\langle 1|-2\langle X|\big)\,.

(173)

Substituting (
172
) and (
173
) into (
171
) gives the explicit form of
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}
in (
170
).

The nondegeneracy of
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt
follows from the existence of its inverse
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}
, satisfying
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}{\hskip 1.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral{I}\hskip 1.0pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt}
.

∎

The nondegeneracy of
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt
is also intuitively expected. In the limiting case
W{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}const
and for metric configurations with homogeneous spatial volume density
\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}\overline{\hskip 1.13809pt\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}\hskip 1.13809pt}(t)
, the operator becomes the identity,
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt\to{\hskip 1.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral{I}\hskip 1.0pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt}
, (
169
), and is therefore full-rank. By continuity of eigenvalues, nondegeneracy holds at least in a neighborhood of such configurations.
The constructive proof above confirms that
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt
is nondegenerate for any sign-definite, bounded function
W
.

For the operator
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt
given by (
163
),
the basic actions of its inverse
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}
from (
170
) are:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lllll}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|1\rangle&=&\frac{|W^{-1}\rangle}{\overline{W^{-1}}\vphantom{|^{I^{I}}}}\,,\\
\langle 1|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}&=&\langle 1|\frac{\langle W|}{\overline{W}\vphantom{|^{I^{I}}}}\,,\end{array}\qquad\qquad\begin{array}[]{lllll}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|W^{-1}\rangle&=&\big({-}|1\rangle+2\frac{|W^{-1}\rangle}{\overline{W^{-1}}\vphantom{|^{I^{I}}}}\big)\frac{1}{\,\overline{W}\,\vphantom{|^{I^{I}}}}\,,\\
\langle W|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}&=&\frac{1}{\,\overline{W^{-1}}\,\vphantom{|^{I^{I}}}}\big({-}\langle 1|+2\frac{\langle W|}{\overline{W}\vphantom{|^{I^{I}}}}\big)\vphantom{\big|^{I^{I}}}\,.\end{array}

Projectors
{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}
and their properties

In the dynamic and gauge structures of GUMG, where
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}
is varied, two symmetric projectors arise,
\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}
and
\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}
, whose basic properties we prove below.

Corollary A.1.1

(Projectors
{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}
)
.

Define two operators:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lllllll}\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}&\!\defeq&{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}W\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}&=&{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}\big(\,W-|W\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle 1|W\rangle}\langle W|\,\big)\,,\\
\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}&\!\defeq&\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}W^{-1}\!{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}&=&\!\!\big(W^{-1}\!-|W^{-1}\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle 1|W^{-1}\rangle}\langle W^{-1}|\big){\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}\,.\\
\end{array}

The operators
\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}
and
\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}
are
symmetric
and
degenerate
operators

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lllllll}\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}&\!=&{W}-|W\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle 1|W\rangle}\langle W|\,,\\
\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}&\!=&\!{W^{-1}}\!-|W^{-1}\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle 1|W^{-1}\rangle}\langle W^{-1}|\,,\\
\end{array}

with left and right kernels consisting of constant functions, so that

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lllllll}\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}|1\rangle=\langle 1|\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}=0\,;\\
\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}|1\rangle=\langle 1|\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}=0\,,\\
\end{array}\qquad\qquad\begin{array}[]{lll}\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}={\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}=\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}\,;\\
\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}={\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}=\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}\,.\\
\end{array}

Proof.

The forms on the right-hand side of (
A.1.1
) directly follow from the expanded representation of
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}
in (
170
), simplified using the identities
{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{W}|W^{-1}\rangle{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}\langle W|{W^{-1}}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}0
.

The symmetric representations (
A.1.1
) and properties (
A.1.1
) follow from the chain of identities

{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}\big(f-|f\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle 1|f\rangle}\langle f|\big)\;=\;{\hskip 1.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral{I}\hskip 1.0pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt}\big(f-|f\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle 1|f\rangle}\langle f|\big)\;=\;\,f-|f\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle 1|f\rangle}\langle f|\,\;=\;\big(f-|f\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle 1|f\rangle}\langle f|\big){\hskip 1.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral{I}\hskip 1.0pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt}\;=\;\big(f-|f\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle 1|f\rangle}\langle f|\big){\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}\,,

provided
{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\overline{\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.4pt}\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.0pt}\,\big(f-|f\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle 1|f\rangle}\langle f|\big)\>=\>\big(f-|f\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle 1|f\rangle}\langle f|\big)\,{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\overline{\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.4pt}\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.0pt}=0
.
These in turn follow from the kernel conditions (
A.1.1
):
\langle 1|\,\big(f-|f\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle 1|f\rangle}\langle f|\big)\>=\>\big(f-|f\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle 1|f\rangle}\langle f|\big)\,|1\rangle=0
, which are verified by direct check.

∎

Also, from the definitions of the projector forms
{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}
, it follows that

\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt\,W^{-1}\,=\;{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}\;=\>W\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}\>.

(190)

Corollary A.1.2
.

The equation
\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}|h\rangle{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}0
on
|h\rangle
is equivalent to
{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}|h\rangle{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}0\,
:

\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}|h\rangle\;\equiv\;{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}W\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|h\rangle\;=\>0\quad\;\;\Leftrightarrow\;\;\quad{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}|h\rangle=0\,.

(191)

Analogously, the equation
\langle g|\,\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}0
on
\langle g|
is equivalent to
\langle g|{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}0\,
:

\langle g|\,\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}\;\equiv\;\langle g|\,\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}W^{-1}\!{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}\;=\>0\quad\;\;\Leftrightarrow\;\;\quad\langle g|{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}=0\,.

(192)

Proof.

The identities
{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}
from
(
A.1.1
)
imply that
{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}|h\rangle{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}0
and
\langle g|{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}0
lead to
{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}|h\rangle=0
and
\langle g|{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}=0
, respectively.

The converse follows from the rank properties: since (symmetric) operators
{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}
and
{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}
have the same rank and kernels of one operator contain kernels of another, their kernels coincide.

To clarify, note that
{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}\defeq\big(f-|f\rangle\frac{1}{\langle 1|f\rangle}\langle f|\big)
for a non-zero
f
is a one-mode-degenerate operator, and it can be extended to the nondegenerate operator with the quantity of rank
1
from the kernel of
{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}\,
:

{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}\;\to\;\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}^{\prime}\defeq{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}{}+{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\overline{\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.4pt}\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.0pt}\;=\;f-|f\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle 1|f\rangle}\langle f|\,+\,|1\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle 1|1\rangle}\langle 1|\,.

(193)

Crucially,
{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}^{\prime}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}
and
{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}^{\prime}
, where
\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}^{\prime}
is nondegenerate and invertible.
Then:
\,{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}^{\prime-1}\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}^{\prime}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}=\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}^{\prime-1}{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}^{\prime-1}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}
and, analogously,
{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}^{\prime}\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}^{\prime-1}={\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}^{\prime-1}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}^{\prime-1}
,
where we used
{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}
from Corollary
A.1.1
.
Therefore,
{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}|h\rangle{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}0
and
\langle g|{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}0
imply
{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}|h\rangle{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}0
and
\langle g|{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}0
respectively.

Together with the first obvious statement of the proof, this implies in each pair that two equations follow from each other, which finally proves their equivalence: (
191
) and (
192
).

∎

This expresses the simple observation that the kernels of both operators are spanned by homogeneous functions, while in the average-free subspace, both operators are nondegenerate.

These properties are used to simplify the constraint
{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}W\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\pi_{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}\rangle{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}0\>\to\>{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}|\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\pi_{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}\rangle{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}0
in Section
4.2
,
and the equation of motion

\langle\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!}F^{-1}|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}W^{-1}\!{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}0\>\to\>\langle\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!}F^{-1}|{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}0
in Section
4.3
.
In Section
4.4
, the operators
{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}
and their properties are utilized to derive the gauge structure.

Variations of
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}

Although the operators
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt
and
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}
are nondegenerate, their variations with respect to the metric acquire a degenerate character.

Lemma A.2

(Variation of
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}
)
.

Under a general metric variation,

\begin{array}[]{lll}\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}&\!=&-\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}\!\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0ptW^{-1}\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}-\,\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0ptW\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}\,.\\
\end{array}

(194)

Proof.

The variation of
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt
is

\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt\,=\,\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0ptW^{-1}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}W+W^{-1}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0ptW\,=\,\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0ptW^{-1}\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt\,+\,\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0ptW,
from which it follows that

\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}\,=\,-\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}(\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt)\,\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}-\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}\!\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0ptW^{-1}\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}-\,\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0ptW\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}\,.

∎

Corollary A.2.1
.

\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|1\rangle\;=\;-\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0ptW\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|1\rangle\,.

(195)

Proof.

Follows from Lemma
A.2
and the property
\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}|1\rangle=0
, (
A.1.1
).

∎

This result allows expressing the variation

\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt{\big(|W^{-1}\rangle/\overline{W^{-1}}\big)}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}-\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0ptW|W^{-1}\rangle/\overline{W^{-1}}

in Section
4.4
, clarifying
the emergence of
\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}
in the constraints on unrestricted gauge parameters.

Lemma A.3

(Poisson brackets of
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}
)
.

\begin{array}[]{lll}{\mathchoice{\big\{{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|e\rangle},{\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle}\big\}}{\big\{{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|e\rangle},{\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle}\big\}}{\{{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|e\rangle},{\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle}\}}{\{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|e\rangle,\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle\}}}&=&-\overline{{f}\,\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}{W^{-1}}}\,\langle h\tfrac{1}{F}\Theta\hskip 0.5pt\pi\hskip 0.5pt|\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}e\rangle+\langle f\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}|h\tfrac{1}{F}\Theta\hskip 0.5pt\pi\hskip 0.5pt\rangle\,\overline{{W}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}{e}}\,,\\
{\mathchoice{\big\{{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|e\rangle},{\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle}\big\}}{\big\{{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|e\rangle},{\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle}\big\}}{\{{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|e\rangle},{\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle}\}}{\{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|e\rangle,\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle\}}}&=&\overline{{f}\,\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}{W^{-1}}}\,\langle\eta^{n}_{\,;{n}}{}\tfrac{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln W}}{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}}|\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}e\rangle-\langle f\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}|\eta^{n}_{\,;{n}}{}\tfrac{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln W}}{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}}\rangle\,\overline{{W}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}{e}}\,,\\
\end{array}

(196)

where
\Theta({\scalebox{0.8}{\mbox{\!$\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}$}}}){\,\defeq\,}\tfrac{1}{\mathrm{d}{-}2}\tfrac{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln{W}}}{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln{\!\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}}}\,\tfrac{F}{\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}

and
\eta^{n}_{\,;{n}}{}\tfrac{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln W}}{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}}=\eta^{n}\partial_{n}\ln W+(\partial\eta)\tfrac{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln W}}{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}}
.

Proof.

From (
194
),

\begin{array}[]{lll}{\mathchoice{\big\{{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|e\rangle},{\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle}\big\}}{\big\{{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|e\rangle},{\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle}\big\}}{\{{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|e\rangle},{\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle}\}}{\{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|e\rangle,\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle\}}}&=&-\langle f\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|{\mathchoice{\big\{{W^{-1}},{\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle}\big\}}{\big\{{W^{-1}},{\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle}\big\}}{\{{W^{-1}},{\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle}\}}{\{W^{-1},\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle\}}}|\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}e\rangle-\langle f\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}|{\mathchoice{\big\{{W},{\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle}\big\}}{\big\{{W},{\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle}\big\}}{\{{W},{\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle}\}}{\{W,\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle\}}}|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}e\rangle\\
&=&-\langle f\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|hW^{-1}\,\tfrac{1}{F}\Theta\hskip 0.5pt\pi\hskip 0.5pt|\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}g\rangle+\langle f\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}|hW\,\tfrac{1}{F}\Theta\hskip 0.5pt\pi\hskip 0.5pt|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}e\rangle\\
&=&-\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|W^{-1}\rangle\frac{1}{{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}V}}\langle h\tfrac{1}{F}\Theta\hskip 0.5pt\pi\hskip 0.5pt|\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}e\rangle+\langle f\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}|h\tfrac{1}{F}\Theta\hskip 0.5pt\pi\hskip 0.5pt\rangle\frac{1}{{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}V}}\langle W|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|e\rangle\,,\end{array}

where we used (
E
) and cancelled the opposite contributions of
\langle f\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}|{h\tfrac{1}{F}\Theta\hskip 0.5pt\pi\hskip 0.5pt}|\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}e\rangle
, extracted from each term in the middle expression.

Similarly, from (
194
),

\begin{array}[]{lll}{\mathchoice{\big\{{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|e\rangle},{\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle}\big\}}{\big\{{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|e\rangle},{\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle}\big\}}{\{{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|e\rangle},{\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle}\}}{\{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|e\rangle,\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle\}}}&=&-\langle f\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|{\mathchoice{\big\{{W^{-1}},{\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle}\big\}}{\big\{{W^{-1}},{\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle}\big\}}{\{{W^{-1}},{\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle}\}}{\{W^{-1},\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle\}}}|\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}e\rangle-\langle f\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}|{\mathchoice{\big\{{W},{\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle}\big\}}{\big\{{W},{\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle}\big\}}{\{{W},{\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle}\}}{\{W,\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle\}}}|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}e\rangle\\
&=&\langle f\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}W^{-1}|\eta^{n}_{\,;{n}}{}\tfrac{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln W}}{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}}|\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}e\rangle-\langle f\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}|\eta^{n}_{\,;{n}}{}\tfrac{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln W}}{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}}|W\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}e\rangle\\
&=&\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|W^{-1}\rangle\frac{1}{{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}V}}\langle 1|\eta^{n}_{\,;{n}}{}\tfrac{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln W}}{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}}|\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}e\rangle-\langle f\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}|\eta^{n}_{\,;{n}}{}\tfrac{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln W}}{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}}|1\rangle\frac{1}{{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}V}}\langle W|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|e\rangle\,,\end{array}

where we used (
E
) and cancelled the opposite contributions of

\langle f\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}|{\eta^{n}_{\,;{n}}{}\tfrac{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln W}}{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}}}|\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}e\rangle
,
extracted from each term in the middle expression.

∎

Corollary A.3.1
.

When
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}
acts to the right on a spatially homogeneous function, the Poisson brackets (
196
) simplify:

\begin{array}[]{lll}{\mathchoice{\big\{{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|1\rangle},{\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle}\big\}}{\big\{{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|1\rangle},{\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle}\big\}}{\{{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|1\rangle},{\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle}\}}{\{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|1\rangle,\langle h|{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}\rangle\}}}&\!=&\langle f\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}|h\tfrac{1}{F}\Theta\hskip 0.5pt\pi\hskip 0.5pt\rangle\,\frac{1}{\overline{W^{-1}}\vphantom{|^{I^{I}}}}\,,\\
{\mathchoice{\big\{{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|1\rangle},{\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle}\big\}}{\big\{{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|1\rangle},{\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle}\big\}}{\{{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|1\rangle},{\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle}\}}{\{\langle f|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|1\rangle,\langle\eta^{n}|{H}_{n}\rangle\}}}&\!=&-\langle f\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}|\eta^{n}_{\,;{n}}{}\tfrac{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln W}}{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}}\rangle\,\frac{1}{\overline{W^{-1}}\vphantom{|^{I^{I}}}}\,.\\
\end{array}

(197)

Proof.

This follows from Lemma
A.3
, along with
\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{+}}}|1\rangle{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}0
, (
A.1.1
),  and
\overline{{W}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}{1}}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}{1}/\,{\overline{W^{-1}}\vphantom{|^{I^{I}}}}
, (
A.1
).

∎

Determinant of operator
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt

Lemma A.4

(Determinant of
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt
)
.

The determinant of the spatial integral operator
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt
, (
163
), with the two-point kernel
{E}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}t|{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}};{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}}^{\prime})
, (
123
), reads:

\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.392,0}\mathrm{Det}}_{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt\,(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}t)\;=\;\overline{W}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}t)\,\overline{W^{-1}}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}t)\geq 1\,,

(198)

The equality is satisfied only for
W(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}t,{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}}){\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}\overline{W}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}t)
, which happen either when
W{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}const
or for metric configurations with
\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}t,{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}}){\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}\overline{\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}t)
.

Proof.

The variation of the determinant gives:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt\ln\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.392,0}\mathrm{Det}}_{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}}}{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt}

\displaystyle\!=\!

\displaystyle\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.392,0}\mathrm{Tr}}_{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}}}\ln\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt\;=\;\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.392,0}\mathrm{Tr}}_{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}}}{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}\,\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt}\;=\;-\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.392,0}\mathrm{Tr}}_{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}}}{\big(\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}{W}^{-1}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\overline{\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.4pt}\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.0pt}\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt{W}\,\big)}-\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.392,0}\mathrm{Tr}}_{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}}}{\big(\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt{W^{-1}}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\overline{\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.4pt}\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.0pt}W\,\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}\big)}

\displaystyle\!=\!

\displaystyle-\big[\tfrac{1}{{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}V}}\langle\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt{W}|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|W^{-1}\rangle\big]-\big[\tfrac{1}{{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}V}}\langle W|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}|\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt{W^{-1}}\rangle\big]\;=\;\tfrac{\langle\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0ptW|1\rangle}{\langle 1|W\rangle}+\tfrac{\langle 1|\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0ptW^{-1}\rangle}{\langle W^{-1}|1\rangle}\;=\;\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt{\ln{\big(\,\overline{W}\,\overline{W^{-1}}\,\big)}}\,.

This implies
\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.392,0}\mathrm{Det}}_{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}}}{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}const\,\overline{W}\,\overline{W^{-1}}
. The constant is fixed by the limit
W\to\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}
, where
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt
is the identity operator.

∎

The result (
198
) can also be checked via Taylor expansion of
\,\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.392,0}\mathrm{Tr}}_{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}}}\ln({\hskip 1.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral{I}\hskip 1.0pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt}+\Delta)\,
in powers of
\,\Delta{\,\defeq\>}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\overline{\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.4pt}\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.0pt}{-\,}W^{-1}\!{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\overline{\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.4pt}\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.0pt}W
, which after taking the trace gives the expansion of
\ln\big(1-(1{-}\overline{W}\,\overline{W^{-1}})\big)
.

A.2
Properties of a generalized delocalization operator and its inverse

More general operators

\displaystyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}

\displaystyle\!=\!

\displaystyle{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\overline{\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.4pt}\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.0pt}+a^{-1\!}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}b^{-1}\;=\;a^{-1\!}{\hskip 1.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral{I}\hskip 1.0pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt}b^{-1}+|1\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle 1|1\rangle}\langle 1|-|a^{-1}\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle 1|1\rangle}\langle b^{-1}|\,,

(199)

defined on compact spaces for arbitrary sign-definite functions
a({\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}})
and
b({\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}})
possess similar properties, except for the homogeneity limit property (
169
), which for
a({\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}})\to\overline{a}
and
b({\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}})\to\overline{b}
with
\overline{b}\neq\overline{a}^{-1}
leads to a rescaling in the inhomogeneous sector. Its featured actions are

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lllll}\langle 1|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}&\!=&\langle 1|+\langle\hskip 0.5pt\widetilde{a^{-1}}b^{-1}|\,,\\
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}|1\rangle&\!=&|1\rangle+|a^{-1}\widetilde{b^{-1}}\rangle\,,\end{array}\qquad\qquad\begin{array}[]{lllll}\langle a|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}&\!\!=&\overline{a}\,\langle 1|\,,\\
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}|b\rangle&\!\!=&|1\rangle\,\overline{b}\vphantom{\big|^{I}}\,.\end{array}

Lemma A.5

(Nondegenerateness and inverse of
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}
)
.

Operator
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\overline{\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.4pt}\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.0pt}+a^{-1}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}b^{-1}
, (
199
),
where
a({\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}})
and
b({\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}})
are bounded, sign-definite functions,
is a
nondegenerate
operator
with an
inverse
of the form

\displaystyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}^{-1}

\displaystyle\!=\!

\displaystyle b{\hskip 1.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral{I}\hskip 1.0pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt}a\;-\;|ba\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle a|1\rangle}\langle a|\;-\;|b\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle b|1\rangle}\langle ba|\;+\;|b\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle b|1\rangle}\big(\langle 1|1\rangle{+}\langle ba|1\rangle\big)\tfrac{1}{\langle a|1\rangle}\langle a|\,.

(205)

From sign-definiteness and boundedness, it follows that
a,b\neq 0,\infty
, and that the integrals
\langle a|1\rangle
and
\langle b|1\rangle
exist and do not vanish.
The nondegeneracy of
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}
is established by the explicit construction of its inverse,
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}^{-1}
, mirroring the approach used in Lemma
A.1
.
The correctness of the expression (
205
) for the inverse operator can also be verified directly.

Corollary A.5.1
.

For the operator
\,\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}={\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\overline{\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.4pt}\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.0pt}{+\,}a^{-1\!}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}b^{-1}
, (
199
), the following properties hold:

•

\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}^{-1}|1\rangle
is independent of
\,a
and takes the form

\;\displaystyle\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}^{-1}|1\rangle=|b\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle b|1\rangle}\langle 1|1\rangle=\tfrac{|b\rangle}{\overline{b}\vphantom{I^{l}}}\,.

•

\langle 1|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}^{-1}
is independent of
\,b
and takes the form

\;\displaystyle\langle 1|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}^{-1}=\langle 1|1\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle a|1\rangle}\langle a|=\tfrac{\langle a|}{\overline{a}}\,.

•

\langle 1|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}^{-1}|1\rangle
is independent of both
\,a
and
\,b
and equals

\;\displaystyle\langle 1|\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}^{-1}|1\rangle=\langle 1|1\rangle\,.

Corollary A.5.2
.

For the operator
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}={\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\overline{\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.4pt}\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.0pt}{+\,}a^{-1\!}{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}b^{-1}
, (
199
), the following holds:

\displaystyle{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}b^{-1}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}^{-1}

\displaystyle\!=\!

\displaystyle{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}a-{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}|a\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle a|1\rangle}\langle a|\;=\;a-|a\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle a|1\rangle}\langle a|\;=\;a{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}{}-|a\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle a|1\rangle}\langle a|{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}\,.

(206)

This is analogous to the properties of operators
{\hskip 0.4pt\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt}
discussed in Corollary
A.1.1
. The first form arises because the last two terms in
b^{-1}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}^{-1}
, (
205
), are of the form
|1\rangle\,..\,\langle\,.\,|
and are annihilated by the inhomogeneous projector
{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}
acting from the left. The subsequent two forms follow from the fact that the rank and the left and right kernels of
a-|a\rangle\tfrac{1}{\langle a|1\rangle}\langle a|
coincide with those of its product with the inhomogeneous projector
{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}
.

Corollary A.5.3
.

The equation
{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}b^{-1}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}^{-1}|f\rangle{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}0\,
for
\,|f\rangle\,
is equivalent to
{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}|f\rangle{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}0\,
:

\displaystyle{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}b^{-1}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.8pt_{\hskip-0.5ptab\hskip-0.2pt}^{-1}|f\rangle=0\quad\;\;\Leftrightarrow\;\;\quad{\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral\hskip-0.5pt\hskip-1.70717pt\widetilde{\hskip 1.70717pt\hskip 1.6pt{I}\hskip 0.3pt\hskip 1.70717pt}\hskip-1.70717pt\pdfliteral}\hskip 2.1pt}|f\rangle=0\,.

(207)

This result is analogous to Corollary
A.1.2
, and the proof follows similarly. The right kernels of both operators are spanned by constant (homogeneous) functions, while in the average-free functional subspace, both operators are nondegenerate.

Appendix B
Calculational Notes on Gauge Symmetries

B.1
\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}
-GUMG canonical gauge transformations in GR-like basis

To prepare for the Lagrangian reduction along the lines of general relativity in Section
4.2
we redefine the configuration space of the canonical system (
60
) via
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\lambda}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}^{-1}
and
\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\pi_{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\varLambda_{\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.1,0.15}\mathbf{0}}}
. This manifests all ADM components of the spacetime metric. Starting from gauge transformations (
84
), we derive
{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt}_{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon,\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!}
and substitute
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\lambda}\to\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!}
,
\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\pi_{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}\to\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\varLambda_{\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.1,0.15}\mathbf{0}}}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\pi_{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}
. Thus we obtain the gauge transformations induced by canonical generators (
83
) for the metric fields of the action (
3.2
):

\left|\begin{array}[]{lll}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt}_{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon,\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta}\gamma_{{n}{m}}&\!=&\big(\gamma_{{k}{m}}\partial_{n}+\gamma_{{n}{k}}\partial_{m}+\partial_{k}\gamma_{{n}{m}}\big)\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta_{{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}(\hskip-1.0ptt\hskip-1.0pt)}}^{{k}}+\tfrac{2}{\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}\left(\pi_{{m}{n}}-\frac{1}{\mathrm{d}{-}2}\,\hskip 0.5pt\pi\hskip 0.5pt\,\gamma_{{m}{n}}\right)F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}\overline{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon}\,,\\

\displaystyle\;=\;{\mathchoice{\big\{{\pi^{{n}{m}}},{{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}({H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}{+}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}_{par^{\prime\prime}}}}{\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!}}}\,\overline{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon}}}\,.

We added the identity
0={\mathchoice{\big\{{\pi^{{n}{m}}},{{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\varLambda_{\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.1,0.15}\mathbf{0}}}}\big\}}{\big\{{\pi^{{n}{m}}},{{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\varLambda_{\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.1,0.15}\mathbf{0}}}}\big\}}{\{{\pi^{{n}{m}}},{{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\varLambda_{\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.1,0.15}\mathbf{0}}}}\}}{\{\pi^{{n}{m}},{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\varLambda_{\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.1,0.15}\mathbf{0}}}\}}}\overline{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon}={\mathchoice{\big\{{\pi^{{n}{m}}},{{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}^{-1}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\varLambda_{\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.1,0.15}\mathbf{0}}}}\big\}}{\big\{{\pi^{{n}{m}}},{{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}^{-1}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\varLambda_{\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.1,0.15}\mathbf{0}}}}\big\}}{\{{\pi^{{n}{m}}},{{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}^{-1}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\varLambda_{\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.1,0.15}\mathbf{0}}}}\}}{\{\pi^{{n}{m}},{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}\,}\overline{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon}+{\mathchoice{\big\{{\pi^{{n}{m}}},{{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}}\big\}}{\big\{{\pi^{{n}{m}}},{{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}}\big\}}{\{{\pi^{{n}{m}}},{{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}}\}}{\{\pi^{{n}{m}},{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}\}}}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}^{-1}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\varLambda_{\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.1,0.15}\mathbf{0}}}\,\overline{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon}

to extract a required trivial gauge transformation
\propto\tfrac{\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt{S^{{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}_{par^{\prime\prime}}}}{\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!}}}
. This leads to a transformation generated by
{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!}({H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}{+\,}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}^{-1}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\varLambda_{\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.1,0.15}\mathbf{0}}})

with the (a posteriori) constrained gauge parameter
{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!}\to F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}\overline{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon}
.

Similarly, the transformation
{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt}_{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon,\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{n}
can be cast to the (restricted) GR form (
3.4
) by noticing that

F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}\gamma^{{n}{m}}\partial_{m}(F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}^{-1}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!})F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}\overline{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon}=-\gamma^{{n}{m}}\big(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!}\hskip 1.0pt\accentset{\leftrightarrow}{\partial}\hskip-1.0pt_{m}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}\overline{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon}\big)
, which gives

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt}_{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon,\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{}}{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}t}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta_{{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}(\hskip-1.0ptt\hskip-1.0pt)}}^{{n}}-{\mathchoice{{\big[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}},{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta_{{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}(\hskip-1.0ptt\hskip-1.0pt)}}}\big]^{{n}}}}{{\big[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}},{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta_{{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}(\hskip-1.0ptt\hskip-1.0pt)}}}\big]^{{n}}}}{{[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}},{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta_{{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}(\hskip-1.0ptt\hskip-1.0pt)}}}]^{{n}}}}{{[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}},{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta_{{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}(\hskip-1.0ptt\hskip-1.0pt)}}}]^{{n}}}}}-(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!}\hskip 1.0pt\accentset{\leftrightarrow}{\partial}\hskip-1.0pt_{m}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}\overline{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon})\gamma^{{m}{n}}\,.

(212)

Thus, omitting trivial transformations, for the metric fields one gets the new canonical form of the symmetry with field-dependent gauge parameters
F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}\overline{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon}
and
\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta_{{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}(\hskip-1.0ptt\hskip-1.0pt)}}
:

\begin{array}[]{|lll}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt^{\prime}\!\!}_{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon,\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta}\gamma_{{n}{m}}&=&\big(\gamma_{{k}{m}}\partial_{n}+\gamma_{{n}{k}}\partial_{m}+(\partial_{k}\gamma_{{n}{m}})\big)\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta_{{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}(\hskip-1.0ptt\hskip-1.0pt)}}^{{k}}+\tfrac{2}{\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}\left(\pi_{{m}{n}}-\frac{1}{\mathrm{d}{-}2}\,\hskip 0.5pt\pi\hskip 0.5pt\,\gamma_{{m}{n}}\right)F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}\overline{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon},\\
{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt^{\prime}\!\!}_{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon,\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta}\pi^{{n}{m}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt}_{F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}\overline{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon}}^{{}^{(GR)}}\pi^{{n}{m}}+{\mathchoice{\big\{{\pi^{{n}{m}}},{{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}^{-1}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\pi_{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}}\big\}}{\big\{{\pi^{{n}{m}}},{{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}^{-1}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\pi_{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}}\big\}}{\{{\pi^{{n}{m}}},{{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}^{-1}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\pi_{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}}\}}{\{\pi^{{n}{m}},{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}^{-1}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\pi_{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}\}}}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}\overline{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon}\,,\\
{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt^{\prime}\!\!}_{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon,\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!}}&=&\tfrac{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{}}{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}t}}(F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}\overline{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon})+\partial_{n}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!}\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta_{{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}(\hskip-1.0ptt\hskip-1.0pt)}}^{n}-\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{n}\partial_{n}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}\overline{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon}\,,\\
{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt^{\prime}\!\!}_{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon,\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{n}&=&\tfrac{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{}}{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}t}}\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta_{{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}(\hskip-1.0ptt\hskip-1.0pt)}}^{{n}}-{\mathchoice{{\big[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}},{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta_{{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}(\hskip-1.0ptt\hskip-1.0pt)}}}\big]^{{n}}}}{{\big[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}},{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta_{{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}(\hskip-1.0ptt\hskip-1.0pt)}}}\big]^{{n}}}}{{[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}},{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta_{{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}(\hskip-1.0ptt\hskip-1.0pt)}}}]^{{n}}}}{{[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}},{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta_{{\color[rgb]{0.2,0.2,0.2}(\hskip-1.0ptt\hskip-1.0pt)}}}]^{{n}}}}}-(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!}\hskip 1.0pt\accentset{\leftrightarrow}{\partial}\hskip-1.0pt_{m}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip 0.5ptw}\!)}}}\overline{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon})\gamma^{{m}{n}}\,.\\
\end{array}

(213)

These modified transformations are induced by the canonical generator (
86
),

{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\operatorname{\delta}\hskip-1.0pt^{\prime}\!\!}_{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\varepsilon,\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta}{(\,.\,)}={\mathchoice{\big\{{\,.\,},{{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}({H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}{+}F_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle{(\!\mathrm{\hskip \widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}
, where we used (
197
) from Corollary
A.3.1
.

Decomposing
\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}
into a difference of a local part and a ‘‘longitudinal’’ projector form:
{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}f\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}h{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}fW^{-1}h-{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}fW^{-1}\frac{1}{{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}W^{-1}}{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}W^{-1}h,
and noting that
\eta^{n}_{\,;{n}}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}^{-1}\partial_{{n}}(\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}\eta^{n})
, allows one to write
\,\eta^{n}_{\,;{n}}{}W{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}\eta^{n}\partial_{n}\ln F+(\partial\eta)W\,
and
\,\eta^{n}_{\,;{n}}{}\tfrac{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln W}}{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}}{\,\hskip 1.0pt=\>}\eta^{n}\partial_{n}\ln W{\,+\,}(\partial\eta)\tfrac{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln W}}{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}}
, so that

\displaystyle{\mathchoice{\big\{{{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip Summing the two contributions (
216
) and (
217
), after cancellations, the residual terms are gathered in an integral structure with the
\widetilde{\hskip-0.5pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral G\pdfliteral}\hskip 0.5pt}_{{\hskip-1.3pt\scriptscriptstyle{-}}}
operator. In the local parts, terms proportional to
\partial_{n}\ln F
and
\partial_{n}\ln W
cancel, leaving only divergence terms with divergences, with coefficients summing to the combination
\Omega
, (
26
).
In the nonlocal part of (
217
), integrating by parts in the first term yields:
{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}W^{-1}\big(\eta^{n}\partial_{n}\ln W{\,+\,}(\partial\eta)\tfrac{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln W}}{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}}\big)={\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}W^{-1}\big((\partial\eta){\,+\,}(\partial\eta)\tfrac{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln W}}{{d}{\hskip 0.8pt}{\ln\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}}}\big)

to which we can add a total derivative
{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}W^{-1}(\partial\eta)W
to factor out
\Omega
:
{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}W^{-1}(\partial\eta)\Omega
.
Thus, the sum of (
216
) and (
217
) becomes

E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}{\,},{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta}^{n}{H}_{n}\}}}\;=\;{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}\mathcal{L}_{{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta}}{(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}+{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip (219)

and the nonvanishing contribution of
{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}\mathcal{L}_{{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta}}{\big(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!}F^{-1}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}\big)}
just reflects the noncovariance of the
(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!})\,F^{-1}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}
structure under unrestricted spatial diffeomorphisms.

The third contribution — the Poisson bracket with the
{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}
structure — is the canonical transformation of
(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!})\,F^{-1}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}
generated by
{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}
. It enters the anomaly in the antisymmetrized combination of
\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.1,0,0.2}\zeta}^{\!{\hskip 0.8pt}\scriptscriptstyle{\perp}\!}
. This antisymmetrization cancels the canonical action of
{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}
on
F^{-1}
:
{\mathchoice{\big\{{{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{\!{\hskip (220)

which naturally gathers the nonlocality into the

structure.

Appendix C
Homogeneous Parameterization

In Section
4.1
we mentioned that homogeneously parameterized action

S_{\,\overline{\!par\!}}[\gamma,\pi,\overline{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\tau}^{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}\!,\overline{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\pi}_{\scriptscriptstyle{0}},\overline{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\lambda}^{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}\!,\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N},\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\mu]=\int d\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}t\hskip 1.0ptd{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}}\,\big(\pi^{{m}{n}}\dot{\gamma}_{{m}{n}}+\overline{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\pi}_{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}\,\dot{\overline{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\tau}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}-\overline{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\lambda}^{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}(\overline{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\pi}_{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}{+}\overline{F{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}})-\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}{N}^{m}{H}_{m}-\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\mu^{m}\mathcal{T}_{,{m}}\big)

(221)

is a self-contained equivalent extension of the canonical theory (
31
). Which at the same time allows to complete the absent homogeneous mode of
{H}_{{}^{{}_{\!\perp}}\!}
canonical structure and reinstate the Einstein-Hilbert term in the Lagrangian action. We noted that adding of the inhomogeneous gauge-trivial sector

{\mathchoice{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}\textstyle{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}{{\mathop{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{\int}}}}}\big(\widetilde{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\pi}_{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}\,\dot{\widetilde{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\tau}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}{\,-\,}\widetilde{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\lambda}^{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}\,\widetilde{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\pi}_{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}\big)

(222)

is motivated by the common practice of dealing only with local (functionally-complete) fields which has certain advantages. The same question is how the alternative action would look like if one factors out the auxiliary gauge-trivial sector of inhomogeneous fields.

The answer is straightforward and could be obtained either by repeating the steps of parameterization, or by reducing the trivial sector from the alternative action (
132
). Both these ways lead a model, defined on mixed-type configuration space of local spacetime metric
\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}g_{{\mu}{\nu}}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}t,{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}})
and a pair of homogeneous fields
\overline{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\tau}^{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}t)
,
\,\overline{\!\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\varLambda_{\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.1,0.15}\mathbf{0}}}\!\!}\,\,(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}t)
with the action

\displaystyle S_{\,\overline{\!alt}_{0}}[\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}g,\overline{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\tau}^{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}\!,\,\overline{\!\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\varLambda_{\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.1,0.15}\mathbf{0}}}\!\!}\,\,]

\displaystyle\!=\!

\displaystyle\!\int\!d\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}t\hskip 1.0ptd{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}{x}}\sqrt{|\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}g|}\Big({{}^{{}^{\mathrm{d}}\!\!}R}(\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}g)-\sqrt{\gamma\hskip 0.5pt}\hskip 0.5pt\vphantom{|}^{-1}\!F^{-1}\tfrac{\,W^{-1}\vphantom{|}}{\,\overline{W^{-1}\!}\,\vphantom{I^{|^{I}}}}\,\overline{\!\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\varLambda_{\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.1,0.15}\mathbf{0}}}\!\!}\,\,\Big)+\int\!d\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}t\,\,\dot{\overline{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\tau}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{0}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0.1563,0}V}\,\overline{\!\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\varLambda_{\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.1,0.15}\mathbf{0}}}\!\!}\,\,\;.\quad

(223)

Here
\frac{W^{-1}}{\,\overline{\!W^{-1}\!}\,\vphantom{I^{|^{I}}}}\,\overline{\!\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\varLambda_{\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.1,0.15}\mathbf{0}}}\!\!}\,\,
comes from
\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\mathbb{\pdfliteral E\pdfliteral}^{\,-1\!}\,\overline{\!\color[rgb]{0,0,0.25}\varLambda_{\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.1,0.15}\mathbf{0}}}\!\!}\,\,

and a nondynamical spatial volume factor
